However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [187] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用有限样本保证（finite-sample guarantees）验证检索管道中高召回阶段遗漏的相关项数量很少。核心发现：仅标注候选集（included pool）无法证明遗漏质量（missed mass）的下界，必须对排除集（excluded pool）进行采样；且排除集审计的标注复杂度在零遗漏（zero-miss）场景下是极小极大最优（minimax rate-optimal）。基于此，作者开发了一套精确有限样本审计工具包，可同时验证遗漏质量、转化为召回率、对候选生成器族进行多重假设检验，并生成压力测试证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高召回率管道中，第一阶段（候选生成）遗漏的相关项将永远丢失，但如何用尽可能少的标注来严格保证遗漏量很小，缺乏有限样本有效且最优的方法论。现有方法多依赖渐近近似或仅关注候选集内部，理论上的标注复杂度尚未被刻画。

Method: 1. 理论证明：任何仅标注候选集的审计程序都无法对遗漏质量给出非平凡（non-trivial）的有限样本保证，必须采样排除集；2. 下界证明：在零遗漏场景下，若需高概率验证遗漏少于 m 项，排除集标注量必须达到 O(N₀/m) 量级（N₀为排除集大小），且该下界匹配可达性（matching lower bound）；3. 实践工具：基于二项分布和超几何分布精确反演（而非渐近近似）构建审计程序，支持两池（two-pool）设计、嵌套候选生成器族的同时验证、以及压力测试证书。

Result: 1. 排除了仅依赖候选集内部标签的可能性，确立了排除集采样的必要性；2. 给出了排除集审计的极小极大最优标注复杂度；3. 开发了完整的精确有限样本审计工具包，所有保证在候选生成器与审计规则预指定（pre-specified）条件下成立。

Conclusion: 排除集审计不仅是实际便利的选择，更是理论上的最优选择。所提出的方法可以在标注预算与审查负担之间进行权衡，帮助选择最经济的候选生成器，且所有保证均为有限样本下的精确保证，不依赖渐近近似。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [188] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAPS（Master-Agent Proto-plan System），一种基于分层深度强化学习的无信号交叉口多车协同框架，通过集中式Master生成全局协调嵌入（proto-plan），分散式Worker根据本地观测执行车辆控制。在72种交叉口配置下，MAPS实现了无碰撞导航并显著降低平均通行时间，且零样本迁移至五车场景仍保持94%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法在无信号交叉口协同中面临组合动作空间过大、依赖特权信息、智能体设计僵化等问题，亟需一种既能实现全局协调又不依赖完全集中式信息的分层架构。

Method: 提出分层DRL架构MAPS：1) 集中式Master智能体生成紧凑连续嵌入（proto-plan），编码全局协调策略；2) 去中心化Worker智能体融合该嵌入与局部观测，执行车辆级控制。该设计将战略意图与战术执行解耦，各模块可独立优化。

Result: 在HighwayEnv的72种交叉口配置下，MAPS实现无碰撞导航，平均通行时间显著优于SOTA基线；三车训练模型零样本迁移至五车场景成功率94%，证明proto-plan具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于proto-plan的分层学习为多车协同提供了有前景的框架，通过全局策略嵌入与局部执行的解耦，兼顾了协调效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [189] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于零流判据的两样本检验方法（ZF2ST），通过学习局部错位方向模式来检测分布差异，使用灵活神经网络同时保持统计有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两样本检验在高维复杂数据（如图像）上检测结构化分布变化时能力有限，需要一种既能利用神经网络灵活性又能保证统计校准的方法。

Method: 基于零流判据（ZFD）构建统计差异量；将见证（witness）学习与假设评估分离，分别开发了基于回归和最大化检验力的两种见证学习方法；使用神经网络学习局部错位方向模式作为分布差异的证据。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上的实验表明，ZF2ST在检测结构化分布变化时具有强检验力，同时能够良好地控制第一类错误率。

Conclusion: ZF2ST是一种有效且实用的两样本检验方法，能够灵活利用神经网络处理复杂数据，同时保持统计上的有效校准。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [190] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: X$^3$-OPD 是一种跨模态同策略蒸馏框架，通过文本教师模型向音频-语言学生模型迁移推理能力，并构建三层对称语料库（文本推理转语音、音频事件推理、口语对话推理），显著提升音频推理和思维链质量，同时保持领域迁移下的原有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频-语言模型在听觉感知上进展显著，但在深度逻辑推理方面落后于纯文本大语言模型，主要原因是高质量音频推理数据的稀缺。

Method: 提出 X$^3$-OPD 框架：1) 同策略蒸馏：学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型使用匹配的文本输入和验证答案提供 token 级指导；2) 构建三层对称语料库：文本推理→语音、复杂声学场景下的音频事件推理、包含副语言线索的口语对话推理，将跨模态蒸馏扩展到超越文本可恢复内容的推理。

Result: 在 MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio 和 MMAR 数据集上，X$^3$-OPD 显著提升了音频推理和思维链质量，同时在领域迁移下基本保持了模型的现有能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态同策略蒸馏结合多层次语料库设计能有效弥补音频语言模型在逻辑推理上的不足，且不损害原有感知能力。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [191] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分子构象集成基础模型EnsembleEGNN，通过对每个构象用共享EGNN编码再通过Set Attention池化，在环肽性质预测任务上显著优于序列基线模型（R²=0.538 vs 0.439）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子性质预测通常仅使用单一代表性构象，忽略了分子在溶液中以构象系综形式存在的物理现实，导致预测偏差。作者希望引入构象系综的统计信息来提升预测精度。

Method: (1) 设计EnsembleEGNN架构：用共享等变图神经网络（EGNN）独立编码每个构象，再用Set Attention Block池化系综表示；(2) 在CREMP环肽构象系综数据集上预训练，采用多任务自监督目标（掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重建、成对距离重建）；(3) 与BERT序列编码器进行端到端协同训练。

Result: 从零训练失败（R²=0.005）；预训练模型达R²=0.477/Pearson r=0.699，超越BERT基线（0.439/0.667）；与BERT协同训练进一步提升至R²=0.538/Pearson r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象系综编码为单一热力学信息的嵌入表示可以显著提升环肽性质预测性能，验证了多构象建模在分子性质预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimePNS是一个两阶段框架，通过反事实干预机制识别时间序列分类器中既充分又必要的关键子序列，优于仅关注充分性的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列可解释性方法仅关注充分性（找到足以维持预测的子序列），可能赋予虚假子序列高重要性。然而，真正可靠的解释应同时满足必要性——即这些子序列对模型决策是不可或缺的。

Method: 两阶段设计：Stage I学习可识别的因果生成过程并获得充分性导向的解释掩码；Stage II对时间因子进行反事实干预以获取必要性信号，用其监督时间门控机制，通过抑制非必要成分、强调反事实必要的成分来优化初始解释。

Result: 在合成和真实时间序列基准上，TimePNS更准确地识别了决策关键子序列，并在充分性-必要性权衡上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 融合反事实必要性推理的框架能有效过滤虚假时间特征，生成更忠实、更可靠的时间序列解释。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [193] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows)和Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs)，一种可扩展维度的流模型框架，通过逐步增广状态空间实现变尺寸生成，适用于连续和离散状态空间，涵盖变长序列和变尺寸图生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流的生成模型受限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法处理输出尺寸本身是自由度的场景，如变长序列或变尺寸图生成。

Method: 提出EFlows框架，通过构建一个扩展插值过程逐步增广状态维度；在此基础上提出EFMs，将任意两个时间步之间的映射分解为两个可学习的操作：expand operator（基于当前状态增广新坐标或token）和transport map（沿插值推动扩展状态），两者组合实现联合扩展与去噪。

Result: EFMs可蒸馏为高效的少步生成模型；在离散单纯形上扩展后支持变尺寸图生成和变长序列生成；现有固定画布的流模型是EFMs的特例（expand operator为单位映射）。

Conclusion: EFlows和EFMs为输出尺寸本身是学习得到的可控自由度的场景提供了原则性框架，在连续和离散模态上均有效。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI的浏览器扩展，采用混合机器学习架构（结合Transformer嵌入、语言特征和自定义"诱饵度"评分）检测点击诱饵文章，XGBoost模型在开放数据集上达到91%的F1分数，能在用户访问前后发出警告，并提供诱饵摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助用户避免误导性的互联网点击诱饵文章，超越传统检测方法，提供可解释的预警和文章摘要。

Method: 采用混合机器学习架构，结合基于Transformer的嵌入、语言学特征和自定义"baitness"评分；评估了从经典向量化到LLM嵌入等多种NLP技术；最终基于XGBoost构建模型。

Result: XGBoost模型在开放组合数据集上达到91%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 该工具能在用户访问点击诱饵文章前后发出警告，提供百分比诱饵可能性评分及基于分析指标的解释，并生成一到两句话的文章摘要作为诱饵剧透。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [195] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了单模型多次采样(自洽性)与多模型集成两种方法对语言模型不确定性建模的效果。通过Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验发现，单模型内最多只有一个维度超越噪声，而多模型集成则有四个特征值显著高于噪声基线。自洽性虽能提供准确的逐问题不确定性估计，但无法捕获跨问题结构；只有多样化的集成才能揭示模型未知的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在重复运行中产生的输出变化是否不仅能用于逐问题的不确定性估计（通过自洽性/多数投票），还能揭示跨问题之间的结构相关性——即类似集成模型中的协同翻转模式。

Method: 在MMLU、HellaSwag、GSM8K五个模型家族上，对比两种设置：(1) 单模型在τ=1下运行100次；(2) 24个不同LLM组成的集成在τ=0下各运行一次。使用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验分离信号与采样噪声。

Result: 单模型内最多只有一个维度超过噪声阈值；而模型集成中有四个特征值显著高于噪声边界。与匹配难度的伯努利零模型相比，集成结果在500次蒙特卡洛模拟中最多只出现一次偶然情况。

Conclusion: 自洽性能准确估计逐问题的不确定性，但无法检测到跨问题结构；只有多样化的模型集成才能揭示模型真正不知道的内容。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [196] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JAXBench是一个面向Google Cloud TPU的50个JAX工作负载的基准测试套件，用于AI生成内核优化。它包含17个来自生产模型的算子（如Llama-3.1等）和33个从KernelBench翻译的算子。研究发现，在Pallas这类文档稀疏的DSL中，目标特定的上下文比模型规模更重要：加入TPU文档后正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，Autocomp的束搜索方法达到1.36x几何平均加速比，在手调内核上达到1.60x（接近2.08x的专家上限）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化领域已有严格基准推动进展，但TPU领域缺乏等效的基准测试工具，因此需要为TPU构建原生的基准测试套件以支持AI生成内核优化研究。

Method: 从MaxText库（Llama-3.1、DeepSeek-V3、Mixtral、Mamba-2、AlphaFold2）提取17个生产ML算子，从KernelBench翻译33个算子并设置新问题规模以达成高TPU v6e MXU利用率；8个生产算子附带Tokamax库的手调Pallas内核作为专家上限基线；评估四种基于反馈的方法生成候选Pallas内核。

Result: Gemini 3 Flash上，目标特定上下文比模型规模更重要；加入TPU文档后正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，解决48/50个基准，几何平均加速1.28x；Autocomp束搜索达到1.36x几何平均加速；在手调内核上达到1.60x，恢复大部分2.08x专家上限，但在分页和稀疏注意力算子上仍落后。

Conclusion: 高质量TPU内核优化仍是挑战性任务，JAXBench基准测试套件、评估框架和基线结果已开源以支持社区贡献。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [197] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap是一种无训练框架，通过动态连续验证策略消除联合概率依赖误差（JPDE），并结合草稿引导的解码机制，实现扩散大语言模型在中等置信度区间的高效加速，在MBPP长序列生成上实现53.19倍加速，结合KV-Cache可达105.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的并行解码策略因过度保守的置信度阈值导致冗余去噪迭代和次优推理速度，其根本原因是联合概率依赖误差（JPDE）的存在。

Method: 提出DC-Leap框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态连续验证策略（Dynamic Contiguous Verification），将严格有序的因果约束融入并行解码过程，逐步验证token依赖关系以消除JPDE；(2) 草稿引导解码机制（draft-guided decoding），通过草稿向前跳跃多个token扩展上下文，在推理时保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，DC-Leap在MBPP长序列生成上实现高达53.19倍加速，结合KV-Cache可达105.02倍加速，且生成质量可与之媲美。

Conclusion: DC-Leap是一种训练免费、可靠的dLLMs加速框架，通过消除JPDE和引入草稿引导解码，在中等置信度区间实现了显著的推理加速，同时保持了生成质量。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [198] [CRAWO: Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20490)
*Eugênio Santos,Daniel Maia,Stefano Loss,José Manoel Silva,Aluizio Rocha Neto,Thais Batista,Everton Cavalcante,Nélio Cacho,Eduardo Nogueira,Daniel Araújo,Frederico Lopes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAWO框架，一种基于控制循环和硬件感知的自适应工作流编排架构，通过Kubernetes CRD和轻量级K3s部署，在智能城市边缘环境中实现AI管线的动态负载分配，并在车牌识别场景中验证了其降低延迟和减少云端依赖的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘编排平台主要关注部署自动化和基础设施管理，缺乏对异构设备能力（从低功耗微控制器到加速器系统）的适应性，导致资源分配效率低下，无法在动态条件下实现自适应资源分配。

Method: CRAWO采用控制循环模型，将分配智能与执行分离，通过硬件感知分配器与可插拔多准则决策层，利用实时基础设施指标进行自适应工作负载放置；参考实现基于微服务架构、K3s轻量级Kubernetes发行版、CRD领域建模及专用算子进行状态协调。

Result: 在基于车牌识别的车辆监控场景中，CRAWO实现了更优的工作负载分布，并减少了对集中式云处理的依赖，尤其在延迟敏感环境下表现突出。

Conclusion: CRAWO通过硬件感知的自适应编排有效解决了异构边缘环境中的AI管线部署效率问题，为智能城市等延迟敏感型应用提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: Edge Intelligence has emerged as a key paradigm for enabling real-time applications in smart cities by shifting computation from centralized cloud data centers to the network edge, thereby reducing latency and bandwidth consumption. However, deploying Artificial Intelligence (AI) pipelines across heterogeneous edge infrastructures remains challenging due to the wide range of device capabilities, from low-power microcontrollers to accelerator-equipped systems. Existing edge orchestration platforms primarily focus on deployment automation and infrastructure management, but these approaches are often inefficient and limit the ability to adaptively allocate resources under dynamic conditions. To tackle these issues, this paper introduces CRAWO (Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration), an architectural framework for coordinating AI pipelines across distributed edge environments. CRAWO follows a control-loop-based model that separates allocation intelligence from execution by managing placement decisions, state management, and inter-stage data flows while instantiating services on edge nodes. The framework incorporates a hardware-aware allocator with a pluggable multi-criteria decision layer that leverages real-time infrastructure metrics to enable adaptive workload placement. The reference implementation adopts a microservices architecture deployed on a lightweight Kubernetes distribution (K3s), using Custom Resource Definitions (CRDs) for domain modeling and a dedicated operator for state reconciliation. Evaluation in a vehicle surveillance scenario using license plate recognition demonstrates improved workload distribution and reduced reliance on centralized cloud processing in latency-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [199] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferenceBench是一个面向AI智能体的基准测试，要求智能体在2小时内优化LLM推理速度。测试发现智能体虽能超越朴素基线（8.08倍），但低于简单超参数搜索（11.53倍），且倾向于固守单一框架而非探索多样化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体基准测试多基于预设工作流或狭窄行动空间，即使是开放任务也常可通过检索已知方案和调整少量超参数解决，难以区分结果是真正优化还是记忆性解决方案。需要一个能反映智能体在开放式AI工程环境中真实能力的基准。

Method: 设计InferenceBench基准测试：智能体需部署OpenAI兼容推理服务器并优化LLM推理速度。每个智能体获得目标LLM、一块H100 GPU、优化场景和2小时时间预算。包含三个针对不同推理瓶颈（预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发请求吞吐量）的场景，以及一个综合平衡三者。测试了15种前沿智能体配置。

Result: 智能体可靠地超越朴素PyTorch基线（最高8.08倍），常达到或超过默认vLLM引擎（4.05倍），但低于简单超参数搜索（11.53倍）。智能体枚举了许多相关优化技术，但绝大多数收敛于单一推理框架，仅测试少数配置，剩余时间用于重复测量、修复或优化超参数，而非探索不同策略。

Conclusion: 性能瓶颈不在于领域知识，而在于提出多样化配置、系统评估并提交最佳方案的能力。InferenceBench反映了智能体在开放式AI工程环境中的真实能力，记忆性解决方案只能带来有限改进。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [200] [Workload-Aware Caching for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20495)
*Anas Mohamed,Kaizan Haque,Azal Ahmad Khan,Chetan Sharma,Shuwen Ge,Ali Anwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多智能体系统的工作负载感知缓存驱逐策略，通过结合重计算成本、DAG依赖计数和智能体调用频率三种信号来保留最有价值的缓存条目，在有限内存下实现最高64.7%的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓存驱逐策略仅基于访问历史统一处理所有缓存条目，忽略了多智能体执行环境中特有的结构信号（如DAG依赖）和工作负载信号（如智能体调用频率），导致缓存效率不佳。

Method: 将重计算成本、DAG依赖计数和智能体调用频率三种信号融合为统一评分函数，在内存约束下保留最有价值的缓存条目。

Result: 相比无缓存基线延迟降低最高64.7%，相比最优有限容量基线平均延迟降低31.1%，性能接近无限缓存，准确率持平或超越所有竞争方法。

Conclusion: 工作负载感知的内容缓存与其他智能体系统优化方法（计划级缓存、并行执行）互补，分别针对多智能体管线的不同效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) of specialized agent executions, creating natural opportunities for caching intermediate results across queries. However, existing cache eviction policies treat all cached entries uniformly based on access history, ignoring structural and workload signals uniquely available in agentic execution environments. We present a workload-aware eviction policy that combines three signals, namely recomputation cost, DAG dependency count, and agent invocation frequency, into a unified scoring function that retains the most valuable entries under memory constraints. Evaluated across three multi-agent benchmarks spanning diverse reuse regimes, our policy reduces latency by up to 64.7% relative to the uncached baseline and achieves on average a 31.1% latency reduction over the next best finite-capacity baseline, while approaching the performance of an unbounded cache and maintaining accuracy on par with or exceeding all competing finite-capacity methods. We further show that workload-aware content caching is complementary to other agentic system optimization methods, including plan-level caching and parallel agent execution, with each technique targeting a distinct efficiency bottleneck in multi-agent pipelines.

</details>


### [201] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DecodeShare协议，发现LLM在解码阶段存在跨任务共享的低维子空间，该子空间对决策性能有显著因果影响，并可用于改进激活引导的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM以单组参数处理多任务，但在KV缓存推理的解码阶段，是否存在任务通用的结构尚不明确。现有研究多关注预填充阶段，解码阶段的通用表征机制未被充分探索。

Method: 提出DecodeShare协议，通过识别解码阶段隐藏状态中跨任务一致共享的低维子空间，并通过移除该子空间（仅解码阶段）进行因果干预实验，与预填充导出或随机子空间对比。进一步分析该子空间对激活引导（activation steering）的影响。

Result: 干扰该共享子空间导致的决策性能下降远大于干扰预填充导出或随机子空间；该子空间与常见引导方向存在重叠；解码阶段评估引导向量比预填充代理更可靠；该子空间虽紧凑但具有高杠杆因果作用。

Conclusion: 解码阶段存在一个紧凑但因果重要的跨任务共享子空间，可作为高杠杆因果通道，对理解LLM解码机制和优化激活引导部署有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [202] [Clustered Edge Intelligence: Beyond Just Convergence of Edge Computing and AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20937)
*Chinmaya Kumar Dehury,Boris Sedlak,Alaa Saleh,Ilir Murturi,Lauri Loven,Satish Narayana Srirama,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出集群边缘智能（CEI）框架，将派生智能视为可独立描述、发现、观察、共享、重用和动态聚类的第一类实体，解决现有边缘计算缺乏智能中心化范式的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘智能研究聚焦于两个方向：用AI管理边缘资源和在边缘设备部署轻量级AI模型，但缺乏将派生智能作为独立可管理实体的智能中心化框架，智能无法被描述、发现、观察、共享、重用及跨异构设备动态聚类。

Method: 提出了三层CEI架构，涵盖智能清单、语义知识表示、通信、可发现性、可观测性、生命周期自动化、聚类机制、市场、互操作性和标准化等关键技术维度和研究方向。

Result: 构建了CEI概念框架，明确了使智能成为跨边云连续体可共享、可重用、可独立表示、发现、观察、交换和管理的第一类实体所需的研究维度与使能技术。

Conclusion: CEI是一种愿景式的智能中心化方法，旨在填补现有边缘计算研究空白，推动从信息时代向智能时代过渡，使派生智能成为边缘网络中的核心可管理资源。

Abstract: We are moving from an information age to the age of intelligence. A decade, or possibly less than that, data will not be the gold anymore rather the derived intelligence out of the data and the information we posses from the edge of the network. Existing Edge Intelligence research focuses mainly on two directions: using AI for edge resource management and deploying lightweight AI models on edge devices. However, existing edge computing research lacks an intelligence-centric framework in which derived intelligence is treated as a first-class, independently manageable entity that can be described, discovered, observed, shared, reused, and dynamically clustered across heterogeneous edge devices and applications. To address these research gaps, we introduced Clustered Edge Intelligence, a visionary intelligence-centric approach. The aim of CEI is to make intelligence a shareable and reusable first-class entity that can be independently represented, discovered, observed, exchanged, and managed across the distributed edge-cloud continuum. We present a three layer CEI architecture and examine enabling technologies and research dimensions, including intelligence inventories, semantic knowledge representation, communication, discoverability, observability, lifecycle automation, clustering mechanisms, marketplaces, interoperability, and standardization.

</details>


### [203] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanE是一个针对抽取式LLM的规划框架，包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理，并引入DTI规划器选择最优基座模型及其组合，以降低标注成本并提升任务特定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调数据集规模庞大导致标注成本高昂，且缺乏针对特定任务优化LLM的方法。

Method: 提出PlanE框架，包括数据分解、指令微调和提示推理三个模块；同时引入Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI)规划器，用于选择最优基座LLM及其DTI组合。

Result: 实验从两个视角验证了PlanE的有效性：(1) 同一基座模型在不同数据集上；(2) 同一数据集在不同基座模型上。同时验证了DTI规划器在不同优化目标下的泛化性。

Conclusion: PlanE框架能有效提升抽取式LLM的任务特定能力，降低标注成本，DTI规划器具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [204] [Benchmarking the Personalization Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20471)
*Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Vinay Aggarwal,Yaman Kumar Singla,Shashwat Dixit,Jitendra Ajmera,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SDR-Bench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在“两方博弈”个性化场景中的表现。该基准基于贝叶斯说服框架，包含6,279个真实销售案例，实验发现前沿模型均存在个性化瓶颈，无法显著区分成功与不成功的销售触达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型打破了传统检索-排序方法的库存限制，能够生成连续的消息变体，但现有基准仅衡量发送方适应性（模型服务于同一用户），缺乏对“两方博弈”问题（生成消息是否能使第三方产生预期行动）的可复现评估。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将Kamenica和Gentzkow (2011)的贝叶斯说服框架适配到生成式智能体，并在销售场景中实例化。构建SDR-Bench语料库（6,279个客户成功案例，覆盖22个行业约200家企业），使用时序约束模拟防止未来数据泄露。通过Fortune 100科技企业队列和新手/资深销售代表进行验证。

Result: 前沿LLM和深度研究智能体均存在一致的个性化瓶颈，在Fortune 100科技队列中，没有任何模型能统计显著区分成功与不成功的销售触达。现场部署中，48%的模型生成内容被评为立即可用，与资深专家一致性达到Pearson 0.82。

Conclusion: 现有大语言模型在经典个性化任务中的表现存在根本性局限，不足以在第三方说服场景中可靠地诱导目标行动。SDR-Bench和SDR-Arena为可复现的生成式个性化研究提供了公开基准。

Abstract: Personalization, the act of varying a message to induce action from a specific receiver while keeping sender, channel, and time fixed, has a long tradition in psychology and marketing as a two-party problem in which sender and receiver have independent objectives. Large language models remove the bounded-inventory constraint of classical retrieval-and-ranking approaches by generating a continuum of message variants conditioned on inferred receiver state, raising the question of how well current models perform personalization in the classical sense. Existing LLM personalization benchmarks measure sender-side adaptation, in which the receiver is the same user the model is serving. The two-party question, whether a generated message induces its intended action in a third party, has been investigated only through A/B tests and small-scale human studies that cannot be re-run against a new model on demand. We adapt the Bayesian Persuasion framework of Kamenica and Gentzkow (2011) to generative agents and instantiate the formulation in sales, where receiver actions are routinely logged against the outreach that induced them. We release SDR-Bench, a public corpus of 6,279 customer success stories spanning 22 industries and approximately 200 enterprises, served through a temporally constrained simulation that prevents future-data leakage. Across frontier LLMs and deep-research agents, we observe a consistent personalization plateau and on a Fortune 100 tech cohort no model statistically separates successful from unsuccessful outreach. A field deployment with 12 professional sales representatives validates the framework, with 48 percent of model-generated content rated immediately useful and senior-expert agreement at Pearson 0.82. We release SDR-Arena and SDR-Bench publicly to support reproducible study of generative personalization at scale.

</details>


### [205] [Robust Critics: Defending LLMs Against Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20472)
*Roman Belaire,Arunesh Sinha,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对话评论引导采样（DCGS）框架，通过推断多轮对话中用户每轮的真实意图来增强LLM安全性，形式化对抗性对话为马尔可夫决策过程，使用价值和遗憾评论器在token和utterance级别评分候选回复，在多个基准上超越强基线模型和前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全机制在多轮对话中采用上下文赌博机方式，忽略对话轨迹，无法区分恶意攻击与善意但表述不当的提问，导致安全与可用性难以平衡。

Method: 将对抗性对话建模为马尔可夫决策过程，学习基于完整对话历史的token级和utterance级价值与遗憾评论器，通过动作-价值评论器对候选回复进行推理时重加权，近似指数倾斜基础策略，保证有限候选池中期望回报的改进。

Result: 在CARES-18k、WildJailbreak、Redbench、Harmbench四个基准上，DCGS在对抗性对话任务中超越强鲁棒基线和前沿模型，且无需微调即可迁移到前沿模型提升其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DCGS通过推断用户意图而非固定规则来平衡安全性与可用性，理论保证期望回报改进，实验证明其有效性和可迁移性。

Abstract: When a user asks a language model something harmful, is it a genuine attack or a misunderstood but well-meaning question? This ambiguity is one of the central challenges of LLM safety. A model that assumes the worst harms legitimate users; one that assumes the best is easily exploited. The problem is compounded in multi-turn dialogue, where an attacker's true intent may only reveal itself gradually across many exchanges, yet existing safety frameworks apply a contextual bandit treatment, ignoring the trajectory of the conversation.
  To that end, we propose Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS), a framework that addresses this by inferring user intent at every turn of dialogue. Instead of applying a fixed rule about what is or is not safe, DCGS learns what the user's intent is likely to be based on the full conversational history and generates responses accordingly. Formally, we model adversarial dialogue as a Markov Decision Process and learn value and regret-based critics at both the individual token and utterance (full response) levels, scoring candidate responses via an action-value critic. We prove that this inference-time reweighting approximates exponential tilting of the base policy, guaranteeing improvement in expected return for any finite candidate pool, a property that group-relative objectives do not exhibit. Evaluated on CARES-18k, WildJailbreak, Redbench, and Harmbench, DCGS outperforms strong robust baselines and frontier models on adversarial dialogue tasks. DCGS also transfers to frontier models, improving their robustness without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [206] [Incomplete Prompt Jailbreaks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20473)
*Yeonjea Kim,Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大语言模型在不完整有害提示下的句子补全漏洞（IPJ），分析了吸引子类型如何引发有害续写，发现模型会系统性地延迟拒绝直至句子终止，且通过参数调优训练模型拒绝不完整有害提示不足以泛化，最后识别了两类功能神经元并提出神经元级干预增强防御鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然开放权重的大语言模型已配备针对有害请求的安全防护，但句子补全任务在面对不完整的有害提示时仍然脆弱，这一现象缺乏系统性的定义和实证分析。

Method: 形式化定义了不完整提示越狱（IPJ）现象，通过分析多种吸引子类型来刻画不完整提示如何引发有害续写；采用参数调优方式训练模型拒绝不完整有害提示；进而识别了终止神经元和续写神经元这两类功能神经元。

Result: 模型在句子补全任务中会系统性地将拒绝行为延迟到句子终止；参数调优训练无法在内容领域和吸引子类型上实现泛化；识别了两类功能神经元——终止神经元和续写神经元。

Conclusion: 神经元级干预为更精确和鲁棒的IPJ防御提供了潜在方案，有望弥补参数调优在泛化性上的不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly released as open-weight models with safeguards against harmful requests. Nevertheless, sentence completion remains vulnerable to incomplete harmful prompts. In this work, we formalize this phenomenon as incomplete prompt jailbreaks (IPJ) and provide a systematic empirical characterization of when and how incomplete prompts elicit harmful continuations. We analyze diverse attractor types associated with incomplete sentence continuation and show that LLMs systematically delay refusal until sentence termination. We further demonstrate that training models to refuse incomplete harmful prompts via parameter tuning is insufficient, failing to generalize across both content domains and attractor types. To enable fine-grained control, we identify two functional neurons: termination and continuation neurons. By clarifying their roles in sentence completion, we highlight the potential of neuron-level interventions for more precise and robust IPJ defenses.

</details>


### [207] [VeriSimpl: Robust Optimization Modeling from Natural Language using Simplification-based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20474)
*Sumaya Abdul Rahman,Seckhen Ariel Andrade Cuellar,Ghani Raissov,Mohammad Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VeriSimpl框架，利用简化验证策略让LLM通过求解器生成的诊断查询来验证优化建模的正确性，在多个基准上提升了准确率并提供了高精度的自验证信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言到优化建模的LLM方法虽能生成可执行公式，但难以保证公式正确实现了预期任务目标，需要一种验证机制来确保语义正确性。

Method: 基于简化验证的思路，利用优化求解器生成简化的诊断查询，从约束和决策变量等不同维度进行简化，使LLM能够在固定全局上下文中进行局部推理，从而判断公式与任务描述的一致性。

Result: 在多个优化基准测试上，VeriSimpl相比现有方法在准确率上取得了一致的提升，同时提供了一种新颖的高精度自验证信号。

Conclusion: 简化验证策略能够有效增强LLM在优化建模中的鲁棒性，为自然语言到优化形式化提供了可靠的正确性保障机制。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces can greatly benefit the accessibility and usability of optimization modeling, and recent advances in large language models (LLMs) show promise in automatically translating textual problem descriptions into executable solver formulations. However, a key challenge for existing approaches is to ensure that the inferred formulation correctly implements the intended task, even if it may execute without errors. We introduce VeriSimpl, a solver LLM framework for robust natural-language-to-optimization formalization. Our approach is based on the idea of simplification-based verification, where the optimization solver is leveraged to generate simplified diagnostic queries about a candidate formulation to allow the LLM to tractably reason about the correctness of the formulation with respect to the task description. We present such simplification strategies along different dimensions with respect to problem constraints and decision variables, which allow the LLM to reason locally under fixed global contexts. Evaluations on a range of optimization benchmarks show how our approach provides consistent improvements in accuracy over existing methods, while also providing a novel high-precision self-verification signal.

</details>


### [208] [SonicSampler: Unified Tile-Aware Kernels for LLM Sampling and Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20475)
*Pragaash Ponnusamy,Shivam Sahni,Jue Wang,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SonicSampler是一个统一的Triton内核套件，通过垂直融合完整的采样流水线（包括logit处理、令牌选择和投机解码验证）到单个工作负载感知的执行模型中，支持动态每请求采样行为（如语法约束解码、各种惩罚、温度缩放、top-k/top-p/min-p过滤和投机验证），并完全兼容CUDA Graph。其核心是一种新颖的分层两阶段top-k算法，利用LLM输出的低熵特性，在批处理执行中实现高达10倍（top-k）和16倍（投机解码）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理中的采样实现存在三个核心问题：(1) 仅加速流水线的子集，而非完整流水线；(2) 依赖多次内核启动，效率低下；(3) 假设批次内同质化采样行为，无法支持动态服务负载和CUDA Graph执行。

Method: (1) 设计统一套件，垂直融合完整采样流水线（logit处理、令牌选择、投机验证）到单个Triton内核中；(2) 提出分层两阶段top-k算法，利用LLM输出的低熵特性进行高效大词汇表选择；(3) 支持动态每请求采样行为，保持CUDA Graph兼容性。

Result: 在top-k算法上实现高达10倍加速；在异构投机解码工作负载上，相较于最先进基线实现高达16倍加速。

Conclusion: SonicSampler通过垂直融合和分层top-k算法，在保持灵活批处理执行的同时，显著加速了完整的LLM采样流水线，有效支持动态服务场景。

Abstract: Sampling in LLM inference comprises a combinatorial set of logit processing, token selection, and verification operations for speculative decoding. However, existing implementations either accelerate only subsets of this pipeline, rely on multiple kernel launches, or assume homogeneous sampling behavior across a batch, limiting support for dynamic serving workloads and preventing efficient CUDA Graph execution. We present $\textbf{SonicSampler}$, a unified suite of tile-aware Triton kernels that vertically fuses the complete sampling pipeline into a fixed, workload-aware execution model. Our kernels support dynamic per-request sampling behaviors, including grammar-constrained decoding, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, logit bias, temperature scaling, top-$k$ / top-$p$ / min-$p$ filtering, and speculative verification - within a single batched kernel while remaining fully CUDA Graph-compatible. Central to our approach is a novel hierarchical two-stage top-$k$ algorithm that achieves up to $\textbf{10x speedup}$ over competitive baselines and exploits the low-entropy structure of LLM outputs to enable efficient selection over large vocabularies. Across heterogeneous speculative decoding workloads, SonicSampler achieves up to $\textbf{16x speedup}$ over state-of-the-art baselines while preserving flexible batched execution.

</details>


### [209] [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Multi-Sensor Physical Hazard Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20476)
*Faizan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 五个大语言模型在多传感器物理 hazard 数据评估中存在系统性缺陷：所有模型在多个传感器均未超过各自安全阈值但联合异常的场景下，完全无法产生预警信号（多传感器场景评分近乎为零），而对单传感器阈值违规判断近乎完美。表格化格式无一致优势，ChatGPT-4o 在纯文本格式下表现显著更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大语言模型在多传感器物理 hazard 数据评估中的可靠性，特别是多传感器联合判断能力相较于单传感器阈值判断的差距。

Method: 设置60个场景（分为多传感器联合评估、响应比例性、模式区分三类），以温度0.0调用1800次API，评估ChatGPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、DeepSeek、Kimi、Llama 3.1 8B五个模型，对比表格与纯文本两种输入格式的表现。

Result: 所有模型在多传感器场景下评分接近零（Q2: 0.000-0.208; Q3: 0.000-0.592），而单传感器场景表现优异（Q1: 0.975-1.000）。表格格式无一致优势，ChatGPT-4o在纯文本下显著更好（p=0.001）。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在多传感器联合物理安全监测中存在严重盲区，无法识别多个传感器虽未超阈值但联合异常的危险信号，直接影响了其在物理安全监控系统中的应用可靠性。

Abstract: We present an empirical benchmark evaluating how five large language models assess multisensor physical hazard data. Testing 60 scenarios across three categories - multi-sensor joint assessment, response proportionality, and pattern disambiguation - with 1,800 API calls at temperature 0.0, we find that all tested models consistently produced no precautionary warning signal across the tested scenarios where multiple sensors are simultaneously elevated below their individual safety limits, while achieving near-perfect accuracy on single-sensor threshold violations. All five models (ChatGPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek, Kimi, Llama 3.1 8B) score near zero on Category A multi-sensor scenarios (Q2: 0.000-0.208; Q3: 0.000-0.592) compared to strong performance on single-sensor scenarios (Category B Q1: 0.975-1.000). Structured tabular formatting shows no consistent advantage over plain prose; ChatGPT-4o performs significantly better under prose (p = 0.001). These findings have direct implications for practitioners deploying the tested models in physical safety monitoring systems.

</details>


### [210] [Semi-Supervised Text-Attributed Graph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20477)
*Yurui Lai,Samir Moustafa,Renchi Yang,Tsz Nam Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WSD框架，一种针对文本属性图（TAGs）的统一半监督数据蒸馏方法，通过图-文本协同编码、Wasserstein距离引导的图草图算法和LLM文本合成模块，在保持下游任务性能的同时显著压缩数据规模，实现了GNN和LLM任务上的最优性能-压缩平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAG表示学习方法与LLM结合时面临严重可扩展性瓶颈；现有数据蒸馏方法无法捕捉图与文本模态间的复杂交互，在半监督环境下难以处理标签稀疏问题，且无法生成人类可读的文本属性以支持下游LLM任务。

Method: 1) 提出图-文本协同编码模块，采用双路径编码器（图感知与图无关）在协同自训练框架中生成可靠伪标签并融合互补的图-文本特征；2) 开发基于Wasserstein距离的图草图算法进行理论指导的图压缩；3) 设计低成本LLM文本合成模块，利用基于聚类的关键词提取为压缩节点生成连贯、人类可读的摘要。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，WSD在GNN和LLM下游任务上均实现了最先进的性能-压缩权衡。

Conclusion: WSD框架有效解决了TAGs中图与文本模态的交互难题，在半监督场景下实现了高效的数据蒸馏，为大规模TAG学习和分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: {\em Text-Attributed Graphs} (TAGs) have emerged as an expressive data model for integrating graph topology with rich textual semantics. Existing representation learning methods over TAGs suffer from severe scalability bottlenecks, particularly together with {\em Large Language Models} (LLMs). While data distillation offers a promising data-centric solution, existing methods fail to capture the complex interplay between graph and text modalities, struggle with the label scarcity inherent in semi-supervised settings, and lack the ability to produce the human-readable textual attributes required for downstream LLM-based tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose \algo{}, a unified semi-supervised framework guided by the {\em Wasserstein Distance} (WSD). Grounded in our empirical findings on real TAGs, \algo{} introduces a graph-text collaborative encoding module that utilizes dual-pathway encoders (graph-aware and -free) within a collaborative self-training scheme to harvest reliable pseudo-labels and fuse complementary graph-text features. Furthermore, we develop a theoretically grounded WSD-based graph sketching algorithm and a cost-effective LLM text synthesis module, which leverages cluster-based keyword extraction to generate coherent, human-readable summaries for condensed nodes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that \algo{} achieves a state-of-the-art performance-compression trade-off in terms of both GNN- and LLM-based downstream tasks, enabling effective and efficient TAG learning or analytics.

</details>


### [211] [Beyond Liars' Bench: The Impact of Lie Typology, Depth, and Sparsity on Deception Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20479)
*Amr Moustafa,Max Feser,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了影响大语言模型欺骗性输出检测性能的多个因素（表示深度、探针表达力、稀疏特征表示和训练数据中的谎言类型学），结果表明最优表示深度高度依赖数据集，更具表达力的探针相比线性基线仅有选择性提升，稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐藏状态表现相似，而训练数据和谎言类型学的选择会显著影响可检测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，针对大语言模型欺骗性输出的检测探针在域外场景中表现不佳——在一种谎言类型上训练的探针无法很好地迁移到涉及其他谎言类型的欺骗场景中。因此，需要系统研究影响检测性能的各种因素。

Method: 通过向标准基准训练数据补充包含多种欺骗类型（编造、遗漏、夸大）的数据集，系统分析了表示深度、探针表达力、稀疏特征表示和训练数据的谎言类型学对检测性能的影响，并在七种探针类型上进行了实验。

Result: 实验结果显示：最优表示深度高度依赖于数据集；更具表达力的探针相比线性基线仅有选择性提升；稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐藏状态表现相似；训练数据和谎言类型学的选择会显著改变可检测性。

Conclusion: 欺骗检测是一个高度依赖于表示的问题，训练数据和谎言类型学的选择对检测效果具有实质性影响。

Abstract: Training probes to detect deceptive outputs from large language models is still an open problem. Recent work has demonstrated that detection probes fail especially in out-of-domain scenarios -- training on one type of lie does not transfer well to deception scenarios involving other types of lies. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on how various factors impact detection performance: representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse feature representations, and the lie typology of the training data. To this end, we augment standard benchmark training data with a supplementary dataset containing diverse types of deception, including fabrication, omission, and exaggeration examples. Analyzing these factors across seven probe types, our experimental results show that the optimal representation depth is highly dataset-dependent, more expressive probes provide only selective gains over linear baselines, and sparse autoencoder features perform similarly to dense hidden states. Ultimately, we demonstrate that the choice of training data and lie typology substantially changes detectability, highlighting that deception detection is a highly representation-dependent problem.

</details>


### [212] [Enabling Scalable Topology Inference in Distribution Systems via Constrained Multi-Source Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20480)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Muhao Guo,Jiaqi Wu,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束推理框架，利用异构证据（电气测量、空间和运行约束）精炼公用事业提供的基准拓扑，实现对配电网拓扑的高精度（>95%）重建，并在拥有8000+AMI电表的三个馈线上验证了其可扩展性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网拓扑识别对故障定位、电压分析等至关重要，但实际中公用事业数据异质且不完善，现有方法仅依赖电气相似性或空间记录，在密集馈线和不一致元数据条件下可靠性差。

Method: 将配电网拓扑识别形式化为一个约束推理问题：以公用事业提供的基准拓扑为基础，检测不一致分配，在约束邻域内进行局部重连以确保可扩展性，并通过迭代施加物理可行性约束来生成运行一致的拓扑估计；同时提出基于证伪的可靠性指标来评估推断连接的可信度。

Result: 在三个馈线（8000+AMI电表）上的实际运行数据验证显示，拓扑重建准确率超过95%，计算开销显著低于全局推理方法；纯相关性方法在密集城市馈线中会产生模糊分配，而结合电气测量与空间/运行约束可实现鲁棒可扩展的拓扑恢复。

Conclusion: 融合电气测量、空间约束和物理运行约束的约束推理方法，能够在实际部署条件下高效、高精度地实现配电网拓扑识别，并为公用事业提供可优先验证的可靠性指标。

Abstract: Accurate distribution system topology is essential for outage localization, voltage analytics, and operation of distribution grids, yet maintaining reliable connectivity records remains challenging in practice due to heterogeneous and imperfect utility data. Existing topology identification methods often rely primarily on electrical similarity or spatial records alone, which become unreliable in dense feeders and under inconsistent metadata conditions. This paper formulates distribution topology identification as a constrained inference problem that refines a utility-provided base topology using heterogeneous evidence while enforcing spatial feasibility and physical operational constraints. Instead of reconstructing connectivity from scratch, the proposed framework detects inconsistent assignments, performs localized reconnection within constrained neighborhoods to ensure scalability, and iteratively enforces physical feasibility to produce operationally consistent topology estimates. In addition, a falsification-driven reliability metric evaluates how strongly each inferred connection is supported relative to alternative feasible assignments, enabling utilities to prioritize verification efforts while preserving system-wide observability. The framework is validated using operational data from three feeders comprising more than $8{,}000$ AMI meters in collaboration with a large U.S. utility. Results demonstrate over $95\%$ topology reconstruction accuracy while significantly reducing computational effort compared with global inference approaches. The study further shows that correlation-based methods alone produce ambiguous assignments in dense urban feeders, whereas combining electrical measurements with spatial and operational constraints enables robust and scalable topology recovery under realistic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [213] [Routing Without Training: Controllable-Ratio LLM Offloading via Reliability Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20481)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Kevin S Chan,Su Wang,Christopher Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARGO是一种无需训练的本地-云端协作框架，通过利用本地模型在采样响应中的推理一致性来决定何时信任本地执行或卸载到云端模型，在多种任务和模型上优于其他无训练基线，甚至在某些设置下超越有监督学习的路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地-云端协作方法依赖训练路由器或协作感知微调，将路由行为绑定到特定运行模式，缺乏灵活性和通用性。

Method: 提出CARGO框架：通过提示变体采样估计本地模型响应一致性，采用贝叶斯早停机制实现样本高效的不确定性控制，并通过轻量级部署时校准支持任意目标协作比例。

Result: 在多种推理和问答任务、多个本地LLM系列和规模、预训练和微调模型上，CARGO一致优于其他无训练基线，并在若干设置下超越有监督学习路由器。

Conclusion: 有效的、可适应的本地-云端协作可以直接从本地模型的内在响应行为中涌现，无需额外的训练路由器。

Abstract: Local-cloud collaboration is a practical way to deploy large language models under resource constraints, but existing methods often rely on trained routers or collaboration-aware finetuning that tie routing behavior to a particular operating regime. In this work, we show that such training may be unnecessary: the local model's own inference-time agreement across sampled responses already provides a strong signal for deciding when to trust local execution and when to offload to a stronger cloud model. We propose CARGO, a training-free routing framework that estimates this agreement through prompt-varied sampling, applies Bayesian early stopping for sample-efficient uncertainty control, and supports arbitrary target collaboration ratios through lightweight deployment-time calibration. Across diverse reasoning and question-answering tasks, multiple local LLM families and scales, and both pretrained and finetuned local models, CARGO consistently outperforms other training-free baselines and in several settings surpasses supervised learned routers. These results suggest that effective and adaptable local-cloud collaboration can emerge directly from the local model's intrinsic response behavior, without requiring an additional trained router.

</details>


### [214] [PersonaTrail: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents through Browsing Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20482)
*Seungbin Yang,Chaewoon Ki,Dohyun Lee,Jaegul Choo,ChaeHun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PersonaTrail基准和PACMem框架，用于评估和增强基于原始浏览历史实现个性化网络代理的能力，实验表明PACMem优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能有效评估网络代理从用户原始浏览历史中推断个性化偏好的能力，要么依赖完全显式的提示，要么将历史简化为抽象形式。

Method: 构建PersonaTrail基准（基于真实浏览轨迹评估个性化）；提出PACMem框架，将原始浏览历史分解为事实记忆（总结单次会话）和偏好记忆（提炼重复行为模式），推理时检索相关条目指导导航。

Result: PACMem在两项任务上均持续优于现有基于记忆的基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效弥补了现有基准在个性化网络代理评估方面的不足，通过结构化记忆分解提升了代理从浏览历史中推断用户偏好的能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have enabled web agents to autonomously execute complex tasks. In practice, users frequently provide underspecified instructions, requiring agents to infer the missing context from their raw browsing histories. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this form of personalization, as they either restrict tasks to fully explicit prompts or abstract web interaction history into simplified forms. To bridge this gap, we introduce PersonaTrail, a benchmark for personalized web agents operating in a managed open web environment. By leveraging realistic browsing trajectories as user history, PersonaTrail evaluates an agent's ability to infer user preferences and recall information from past browsing sessions. We further propose Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem), a framework that decomposes raw browsing histories into two types of structured memory: factual memories that summarize individual sessions and preference memories that distill recurring behavioral patterns. At inference time, the agent retrieves the most relevant entries from these memories to guide personalized navigation. Extensive experiments show that PACMem consistently outperforms existing memory-based baselines on both tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Tractable Hierarchical Control of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20483)
*Max Scribner,Antonio Vergari,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多项式时间算法，用于约束自回归大语言模型生成符合LR(k)上下文无关语法的输出，相比先前指数时间方法有显著效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM集成到形式系统中时，需要确保生成代码或数据（如SQL、JSON）的语法有效性。现有方法处理LR(k)文法时时间复杂度为指数级，亟需更高效的约束生成方法。

Method: 将LLM蒸馏为可处理的概率模型，通过掩码和引导机制在自回归生成过程中融入逻辑约束的概率计算。核心贡献是证明了任意有限长度LR(k)文法的满足性可在多项式时间内计算。

Result: 实现了对LR(k)文法约束满足性的多项式时间计算，显著优于先前方法的指数时间复杂度。

Conclusion: 该成果使得LLM生成能更高效地遵循形式语法约束，为代码合成、结构化数据生成等任务提供了可行的语法保证方案。

Abstract: Constraining the generation of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) is an important component of integrating language models into formal systems. In the generation of code and data for tasks like program synthesis, ensuring that language models produce syntactically valid output is a prerequisite for processing such output. These languages (such as SQL or JSON) are often designed as $LR(k)$ context-free grammars. By distilling the LLM to a tractable probabilistic model, its autoregressive generation can be steered and masked to incorporate the probability of satisfying logical constraints, ensuring high quality output that is guaranteed to be valid. This paper demonstrates that the satisfaction of any $LR(k)$ grammar of finite duration can be calculated in polynomial time, an improvement over the exponential time of applying previous methods to such grammars. This result enables efficient constraint and steering of LLM generation towards output that better satisfies formal syntactic constraints.

</details>


### [216] [The Devil is in the Spectrum: Mitigating Representation Collapse in LLMs via Topologically Regularized Side-Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20484)
*Yiheng Tao,Kaiwen Cheng,Yao Lu,Chang Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑正则化旁路（TRSP），通过非侵入式架构干预解决LLM中的表征坍缩问题（均匀化坍缩与孤立坍缩），在注意力动态中实现谱平衡，显著提升长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在长上下文场景中存在表征坍缩瓶颈，标准注意力机制易陷入两种极端：均匀化坍缩（如attention sink导致秩不足）和孤立坍缩（如局部注意力导致上下文断裂），两者的根本矛盾在于混合效率（谱间隙）与信息容量（有效秩）之间存在固有折衷。

Method: 提出拓扑正则化旁路（TRSP），包含两个核心组件：1) 无参数的三角箱（Triangular Box）机制，通过正则化token交互拓扑实现谱平衡；2) 轻量级长度感知门控（length-aware gate）进行缩放。TRSP通过近端耦合保持有效秩，远端传播支持非退化混合，从而构建几何上更优的转移算子，且不修改核心注意力机制。

Result: 在通用能力和长上下文基准测试上均取得显著提升。在NoLiMa基准上，当训练长度扩展8倍时，TRSP保持83%的准确率，分别超越Differential Transformer和Gated Attention约30和50个百分点。

Conclusion: TRSP通过拓扑正则化实现了注意力动态中的谱平衡，有效解决了表征坍缩问题，为非侵入式改进长上下文LLM提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are fundamentally limited by representation collapse, a bottleneck that severely degrades long-context performance. We identify that existing approaches risk drifting into one of two pathological extremes: homogenization collapse (e.g., attention sinks causing rank deficiency) and isolation collapse (e.g., local attention causing context disconnection). Through spectral analysis of attention dynamics, we derive an intrinsic trade-off between mixing efficiency (spectral gap) and information capacity (effective rank) that standard mechanisms struggle to balance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose the Topologically Regularized Side-Path (TRSP), a non-invasive architectural intervention that achieves spectral balance. TRSP employs a parameter-free Triangular Box mechanism, scaled by a lightweight, length-aware gate, to regularize the token interaction topology. By integrating proximal coupling to preserve effective rank and distal propagation to support non-degenerate mixing, TRSP promotes a geometrically healthier transition operator without altering core attention. Experiments show significant improvements across general capabilities and long-context benchmarks. Notably, on NoLiMa at $8\times$ the training length, TRSP retains $83\%$ accuracy and surpasses the Differential Transformer and Gated Attention by approximately 30 and 50 percentage points, respectively. Code available at: https://github.com/Eziotao-tyd/TRSP.

</details>


### [217] [Expectation Alignment of Language Models for Real-World User Expectations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20485)
*Miaomiao Li,Yang Wang,Bin Liang,Shudong Liu,Zhiwei Zhang,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究真实LLM交互中的用户期望，提出期望提取流程和ExpectBench基准，发现当前LLM难以满足和预判用户期望，并提出轻量级隐式期望感知响应生成框架LENS以改善对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（模型启发式、专家评分、用户模拟）无法捕捉真实用户期望的多样性和微妙性，导致模型看似表现良好实则与用户实际需求不一致，亟需系统研究用户期望以实现更好的人机对齐。

Method: 提出从真实LLM交互中提取语义丰富期望的原则性流程；构建ExpectBench基准；提出LENS（轻量级隐式期望感知响应生成框架），使模型内化用户期望并生成更对齐的响应。

Result: 当前LLM在满足和预判用户期望方面表现不佳，揭示了根本性的对齐缺陷；LENS框架持续提升了期望满足度。

Conclusion: 显式建模用户期望对于实现真实的人类-AI对齐至关重要，LENS证明了轻量级期望感知方法的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on standard benchmarks, yet it remains largely unexplored whether they truly meet user expectations. Existing evaluation approaches, relying on model heuristics, expert rubrics, or user simulation, fail to capture the diversity and subtlety of real human expectations, causing models to appear competent while misaligning with what users actually seek. We present the first systematic study of user expectations in real-world LLM interactions, proposing a principled procedure to extract semantically rich expectations and introducing ExpectBench, a benchmark grounded in real user expectations. Analyses reveal that current LLMs struggle to satisfy and anticipate what users hope to obtain, highlighting a fundamental source of misalignment. Building on these observations, we propose LENS, a lightweight latent expectation-aware response generation framework. LENS enables models to internalize user expectations and generate better-aligned responses, consistently improving expectation satisfaction and underscoring the importance of explicitly modeling user expectations for realistic human-AI alignment.

</details>


### [218] [OPTScientist: Multi-Agent Discovery of Typed Optimizer Programs for Transformer Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20486)
*Zhongzheng Li,Tiancan Feng,Wenhao Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OPTScientist，一个基于类型化领域特定语言(DSL)和理论引导的多智能体框架，用于自动化优化器发现。通过演化搜索和DSL扩展机制，发现了一个改进Transformer预训练的减状态矩阵优化器RS-MR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习优化器设计面临优化几何、状态动力学、数值稳定性、实现约束和泛化能力等多方面挑战。现有自动优化器发现方法要么在无约束代码空间中搜索（灵活但易产生无效程序），要么在窄参数化优化器族内搜索（稳定但限制创新性），缺乏兼顾灵活性与稳定性的方法。

Method: 提出OPTScientist框架：(1) 定义类型化DSL，候选更新通过方向、缩放、预处理、正则化、状态和分组六个模块表达；(2) 采用理论家、设计者、工程师、审稿人四个角色智能体协作循环；(3) 结合演化搜索和第二阶段DSL扩展机制（在重复失败表明表征瓶颈时提出小规模DSL扩展）。

Result: 发现RS-MR，一种减状态矩阵优化器，在原生评估协议下相比强基线改进了Transformer预训练效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于理论、类型化程序、编译器验证和闭环实验的自动化优化器科学是可行的路径。

Abstract: Designing optimizers for modern deep learning remains a challenging scientific problem, requiring the joint consideration of optimization geometry, state dynamics, numerical stability, implementation constraints, and empirical generalization. Existing automated optimizer discovery methods typically search either over unconstrained code spaces or within narrowly parameterized optimizer families. The former is flexible but often produces invalid or uninterpretable programs, while the latter is stable but limits novelty. We introduce OPTScientist, a theory-guided multi-agent framework for optimizer discovery in a typed domain-specific language (DSL). OPTScientist formulates optimizer design as a constrained scientific search process, where candidate updates are expressed through direction, scaling, preconditioning, regularization, state, and grouping modules. Four role agents, Theorist, Designer, Engineer, and Reviewer, collaborate within a single orchestration loop to propose hypotheses, synthesize DSL candidates, compile and evaluate optimizers, and critique results. To overcome the limitations of a fixed search space, OPTScientist combines evolutionary search over optimizer programs with a second-stage mechanism that proposes small DSL extensions when repeated failures reveal representational bottlenecks. Using this framework, we discover RS-MR, a reduced-state matrix optimizer that improves transformer pretraining over strong baselines under our native evaluation protocol. Our results suggest a path toward automated optimizer science grounded in theory, typed programs, compiler validation, and closed-loop experimentation.

</details>


### [219] [Directional Hallucinations: Ideological Drift in News-Grounded LLM Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20487)
*Chendi Wang,Liam Cunningham,Tom Yishay,Jieying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可复现的测量框架，通过检测大语言模型在政治新闻文档问答中的幻觉（hallucination）现象，发现幻觉内容存在系统性左倾漂移（leftward drift），且部分模型的不确定性可预测这种漂移方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于回答政治信息问题，尤其在选举相关场景中，事实错误和意识形态扭曲风险极高。现有研究缺乏系统性的方法来检测和量化LLM在政治文本中的意识形态偏差。

Method: 使用QBias数据集（21,727篇专家标注的美国政治新闻，涵盖左、中、右来源），五个步骤：(i) 生成文章特定问题，(ii) 从三个开源模型和一个闭源模型获取基于文档的答案，(iii) 通过参考对比检测句子级幻觉，(iv) 用微调立场分类器对幻觉句子的意识形态倾向进行分类，(v) 分析输出logits以关联token级不确定性与幻觉和漂移。

Result: 幻觉率在不同模型间差异显著，集中在争议性话题；来源意识形态差异对幻觉频率影响较小。但幻觉内容存在稳健的左倾漂移：大多数幻觉句子被分类为左倾，包括从右倾来源生成的幻觉。logit分析表明幻觉出现在高熵生成上下文中，部分模型的不确定性也能预测左倾漂移。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM在政治问答中存在系统性意识形态漂移，提出"不确定性到猜测"（uncertainty-to-guessing）机制解释该现象，对审计AI中介政治信息和设计选举相关部署的安全保障具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer questions about political information, including in election-adjacent information settings where factual errors and ideological distortions are high-stakes. We present a reproducible measurement framework that treats hallucinations, unsupported statements in document-grounded QA, as diagnostic signals of ideological drift. Using 21,727 expert-labeled U.S. political news articles from QBias spanning left, center, and right sources, we (i) generate an article-specific question, (ii) elicit document-grounded answers from three open-weight LLMs and one proprietary model, (iii) detect sentence-level hallucinations via reference-based comparison, (iv) classify the ideological valence of hallucinated sentences with a fine-tuned stance classifier, and (v) probe output logits to relate token-level uncertainty to hallucination and drift. Hallucination rates vary substantially across models and concentrate in contentious topics, while source-ideology differences in hallucination frequency are modest. In contrast, hallucination content exhibits robust leftward drift: a majority of hallucinated sentences are classified as left-leaning, including among hallucinations generated from right-leaning sources. Logit-level analysis shows hallucinations arise in high-entropy generation contexts, and in some models uncertainty also predicts leftward drift, consistent with an "uncertainty to guessing" mechanism. We discuss implications for auditing AI-mediated political information and for designing safeguards in election-relevant deployments.

</details>


### [220] [Autonomous Topology Mutation: Safe Runtime Restructuring for Multi-Agent LLM Systems with Capability, State, and Shadow Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20488)
*Bronislav Sidik,Chaya Levi,Nizzan Kimhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自主拓扑突变（ATM）机制，使多智能体LLM框架能在运行时动态重组团队拓扑。通过遥测驱动的过载检测与三个安全不变性约束，将过载智能体分解为专业子智能体并热替换为协调者角色。实验表明，该方法将代码任务成功率从3.3%提升至61.7%，零高隐私泄露事件，p99延迟增加小于500微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM框架通常在启动时固定团队拓扑结构。当单个智能体在运行时因混合过多动作类别、累积工具错误或排队过多调用而过载时，系统缺乏自我重构机制。现有方法无法动态调整团队结构以适应运行时负载变化。

Method: ATM结合遥测驱动的过载检测与三个安全不变性门控（能力单调性、状态路由完整性、影子优先于实体验证）。监控包含队列深度、上下文颠簸、工具错误率、角色熵、重试循环率和跨智能体等待时间的六信号瓶颈指数。当超过预热校准阈值时，将过载智能体分解为专业子智能体，并将其父智能体热替换为协调者角色。状态转移采用隐私级别感知路由。

Result: 在720次DeepSeek-V3驱动的任务运行中，ATM分解器将代码任务成功率从3.3%提升至61.7%。完整的轨道与蒸馏系统在正则分类器下将检测到的高隐私内存暴露从每任务2.0降至0.0事件，同时保持任务质量。运行时轨道增加p99延迟小于500微秒。

Conclusion: ATM机制成功实现了多智能体系统的运行时拓扑动态重组，显著提升任务成功率并消除高隐私泄露，同时引入的开销极低。该框架已开源，为可自适应多智能体系统的设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM frameworks typically fix their team topology at boot time. When an individual agent becomes overloaded at runtime, for example by mixing too many action categories, accumulating tool errors, or queueing behind too many calls, the system has no mechanism to restructure itself. We introduce Autonomous Topology Mutation (ATM), a runtime team-mutation mechanism for multi-agent LLM frameworks. ATM combines telemetry-driven overload detection with three safety invariants that gate each structural change: capability monotonicity, state-routing completeness, and shadow-before-live validation.
  ATM monitors a six-signal Bottleneck Index that includes queue depth, context thrash, tool-error rate, role entropy, retry-loop rate, and cross-agent wait time. When a warmup-calibrated threshold is breached for multiple consecutive ticks, ATM factorises the overloaded agent into specialised sub-agents and hot-swaps the parent into a coordinator role while preserving its external identity. State transfer is controlled by privacy-level-aware routing: each memory atom is routed only to a permitted child set, or explicitly dropped with a logged reason. No candidate topology receives live traffic until it has passed a shadow validation window.
  On 720 DeepSeek-V3-driven task runs with deterministic tool stubs across four ablation conditions and three workloads, the ATM factoriser split lifts code-task success from 3.3% to 61.7%. The full rail-and-distillation system reduces detected high-privacy memory exposure under a regex classifier from 2.0 to 0.0 events per task while preserving task quality. The runtime rails carrying ATM's invariants add less than 500 microseconds of p99 latency on the agent hot path. A small live-tool probe with real Python execution is included as an external-validity check. The implementation, benchmark harness, and traces are open-sourced.

</details>


### [221] [EvoSQL: Memory-Augmented Critic-Generator Co-Evolution for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20489)
*Jiawei Zhou,Jianwei Wang,Chenyu Zhou,Chaojian Shi,Ming Dong,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoSQL是一个协同进化框架，通过生成器与评判器之间的迭代交互，结合上下文候选记忆、执行信号与LLM评判验证、效用引导聚合以及SDPO微调，显著提升了Text-to-SQL在Spider和BIRD基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂数据库查询需要超越单次生成的推理能力，包括多步分解、基于执行的诊断和针对性修正，而现有方法难以有效整合这些能力。

Method: EvoSQL构建了一个生成器-评判器协同进化框架，维护上下文候选记忆，利用执行信号和LLM评判进行验证，通过效用引导聚合更新记忆；并引入SDPO微调策略注入执行感知监督。

Result: 在Spider和BIRD基准上，EvoSQL一致优于Maj@16基线，BIRD-Dev上提升+1.37%（Qwen3-4B）至+9.19%（Qwen2.5-Coder-3B），SDPO进一步提升了部分骨干模型在Spider-Test和BIRD-Dev上的性能。

Conclusion: 基于记忆的协同进化是构建更可靠和泛化的Text-to-SQL系统的有效路径。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL has advanced rapidly with large language models, but complex database queries still require reasoning beyond one-shot generation, including multi-step decomposition, execution-based diagnosis, and targeted correction. We present EvoSQL, a co-evolution framework that formulates SQL synthesis as an iterative interaction between a generator and a critic. EvoSQL maintains a contextualized candidate memory, verifies SQL candidates with both execution signals and LLM-based critique, and updates its memory through utility-guided aggregation. To strengthen the underlying generator-critic pair, we further introduce a Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) fine-tuning stage that injects execution-aware supervision into modern coding LLM backbones. Experiments on Spider and BIRD show that EvoSQL consistently improves open-source models over Maj@16 baselines, with particularly large gains on BIRD-Dev, ranging from +1.37% for Qwen3-4B to +9.19% for Qwen2.5-Coder-3B. SDPO initialization further improves selected backbones on Spider-Test and BIRD-Dev. These results suggest that memory-grounded co-evolution is an effective path toward more reliable and generalizable Text-to-SQL systems. Code is available at https://github.com/valleysprings/EvoSQL.

</details>


### [222] [DFAH-Bench: Benchmarking Observable Agent Instability in Financial Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20491)
*Raffi Khatchadourian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DFAH-Bench重放基准，从工具调用轨迹、证据接触和决策集中度三个维度衡量金融智能代理的行为不稳定性。发现前沿模型在95%决策一致时，仅有77%的时间遵循相同工具路径（18%差距），并识别出模式匹配器、稳定执行者和轨迹发散者三种行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估基准仅衡量工具使用代理的最终决策，而不关注其是否通过相同过程达成决策，忽略了行为不稳定性的重要维度。

Method: 构建DFAH-Bench重放基准，在8,127次重放实验中，跨10个模型和3个金融任务，从工具调用轨迹、证据接触和决策集中度三个可观测通道评估行为不稳定性。

Result: 前沿模型在95%的决策一致情况下，仅有77%的时间遵循相同工具路径，存在18个百分点的差距。超过55%的高决策一致案例组存在显著轨迹发散。

Conclusion: 仅依赖结果一致性的评估是不完整的，需要从过程层面对智能代理的行为稳定性进行多维度评估。

Abstract: Standard evaluation benchmarks measure what a tool-using agent decides, not whether it arrives at that decision through the same process each time. We introduce DFAH-Bench, a replay benchmark that measures observable behavioral instability in financial agent decision-making across three channels -- tool-call trajectories, evidence contacts, and decision concentration -- none of which require access to hidden reasoning text. Across 8,127 replay episodes spanning 10 models and 3 financial tasks, we find that outcome agreement alone is an incomplete stability signal: frontier models can agree on decisions 95% of the time while following the same tool path only 77% of the time -- an 18-percentage-point gap (95% CI: [0.14, 0.22]) that outcome-only evaluation misses entirely. Among frontier-model case groups with high decision agreement, over 55% exhibit meaningful trajectory divergence. We identify three behavioral profiles: pattern matchers that achieve near-perfect agreement by collapsing to a single output regardless of input, stable executors with relatively consistent tool-use processes, and trajectory divergers that reach the same conclusions through materially different tool paths and evidence contacts. The benchmark code, metric scripts, replay logs, benchmark card, dataset README, and release manifest are released in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [223] [Attention-based Experience Replay Framework for Continual Learning of Agnostic Time Series Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20493)
*Quentin Besnard,Nicolas Ragot*

Main category: cs.AI
